Web App vs Mobile App — Key Differences

The web app serves the same salesman role but provides a larger screen experience for when the salesman is at a desk, counter, or depot — not in the field. Both apps share the same API but the web app has more space for data, tables, and detailed reporting.

Theme: Clean professional — sidebar layout, data-forward, works at 1280px minimum width.
Feel: A business tool not a consumer app — reliable, clear, information-dense but not cluttered.
Sidebar: 240px fixed left sidebar with logo, nav links, and user info at bottom.
Content area: fluid width, max-content 1200px, padding 24px.
Typography: Readable at all zoom levels — important for salesmen who may use large text.

Layout Structure
Sidebar (240px fixed left)
Logo and app name at top — CylinderHub in teal
Navigation links with icons — highlight active page in teal left border + teal bg
User info at bottom — avatar initials, name, role badge
Logout button at very bottom
Collapses to 64px icon-only sidebar on screens below 1024px

Top Header Bar (full width, 64px height)
Page title — matches current page
Breadcrumb on inner pages e.g. Sales > Sale #42
Notifications bell with unread count badge
Language toggle: EN / BN
Today&apos;s date displayed

Content Area
White background, 24px padding on all sides
Section cards: white bg, border-radius 12px, 1px border #D0D5DD, padding 20px
Page header inside content: page title left, action button(s) right

Component Library — Web
Data Tables
Header row: Navy bg, white text, 14px semibold
Alternating rows: white / #F4F6F8
Hover row: light teal highlight #DFF3F5
Sortable columns: arrow icon on hover, filled arrow when sorted
Row actions: Edit / View / Collect buttons appear on hover (right side of row)
Pagination: previous / next + page numbers at bottom
Row click: navigates to detail page
Sticky header: table header stays visible on scroll

Status Badges (pill shaped)

<w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="5360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="CCCCCC" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="CCCCCC" w:sz="1"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="CCCCCC" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="CCCCCC" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="FFFFFF" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="140"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="140"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b w:val="false"/><w:bCs w:val="false"/><w:color w:val="111111"/><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Due payment older than 7 days

Buttons
Primary: Teal bg #0B6E75, white text, height 40px, border-radius 8px, hover darken 10%
Secondary: White bg, 1px teal border, teal text — same sizing
Danger: Red bg #B83030, white text — only for destructive actions
Ghost: Transparent bg, teal text, no border — for inline table actions
Icon button: 36x36px, circular, teal icon on hover

Forms
Label above field, 13px grey text
Input height 42px, border 1px #D0D5DD, border-radius 8px, font 14px
Focus: border-color teal, box-shadow 0 0 0 3px rgba(11,110,117,0.15)
Select dropdown: custom styled, chevron icon on right
Required fields marked with red asterisk after label
Error state: red border + red helper text below field
Inline validation on blur — do not wait until submit

Modals / Dialogs
Backdrop: rgba(0,0,0,0.5) overlay
Dialog: white bg, border-radius 16px, max-width 560px, centered
Header: title left, X close button right
Footer: action buttons right-aligned — Cancel (secondary) + Confirm (primary)
Pressing Escape closes the modal

Page-by-Page Specifications

Page 1 — Login
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 1: Login  ·  Route: /login
Full-screen login. No sidebar. Clean centered card.
Centered card: 480px wide, white bg, shadow, rounded corners
CylinderHub logo + name at top of card
Subtitle: Salesman Portal in grey
Email input field
Password input with show/hide eye icon toggle
Remember me checkbox
Login button — full width, primary teal
Language toggle in top-right corner of page
Error state: red alert box above form with message
Loading: button shows spinner and is disabled
On success: redirect to /dashboard
API: POST /auth/login — saves token to localStorage

Page 2 — Dashboard (My Day)
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 2: Dashboard  ·  Route: /dashboard
The main home page. Full overview of today at a glance.
Page header: Good morning [name] — [today&apos;s date]
Stats row: 4 metric cards — Total Allocated / Total Sold / Cash Collected / Outstanding Dues
Each metric card: icon, label, large number, small % change vs yesterday
Section: Today&apos;s Allocations — one card per cylinder type
Each allocation card: cylinder name + size, sale price, progress bar (sold/total), 4 numbers (Allocated/Sold/Returned/With You), End of Day button
If reconciled: green Reconciled banner replaces End of Day button
Section: Today&apos;s Sales — last 5 sales in a mini-table with See All link
Mini-table columns: Time, Customer, Items, Amount, Payment Badge, Collect button (if due)
Section: Outstanding Dues Summary — total count and total amount, link to /dues
Section: Empty Cylinders Today — total empties collected today per size
Alert banner at top if any allocation is overdue for reconciliation (past 7 PM)
API: GET /salesmen/{id}

Page 3 — New Sale
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 3: New Sale  ·  Route: /sales/new
Full-page sale recording form. Two-column layout on desktop.
Left column (60%): Sale form inputs
Right column (40%): Live order summary — sticky, updates in real time
Customer field: searchable dropdown with autocomplete, Walk-in as default option
Add New Customer inline button — opens a small inline panel with name + phone fields
Items section: table with columns — Cylinder Type, Qty, Unit Price (read-only), Line Total, Remove row
Cylinder type dropdown: only shows types allocated to this salesman today with remaining qty shown
Qty: number input with +/- buttons, max = remaining allocation for that type
Add Item row button below the items table
Payment section: 3 large radio-style toggle cards — CASH / PARTIAL / DUE LATER
If PARTIAL: Amount Paid input appears below, due calculates automatically
Sale date: defaults to today, editable date picker
Notes field: optional free text
Right panel: Customer name, item breakdown, subtotal, paid (green), due (red), big Record Sale button
Keyboard shortcut: Ctrl+Enter submits the form
Success: green toast notification + redirect to /sales/{id}
API: GET /customers (autocomplete), POST /customers (new), POST /sales

Page 4 — Sales History
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 4: Sales History  ·  Route: /sales
Full paginated data table of all this salesman&apos;s sales.
Page header: Sales History title, New Sale button top right
Filter bar: Date range picker, Payment type filter (All/Cash/Partial/Due), Search by customer name
Quick filter tabs: Today / This Week / This Month / All
Export button: download filtered results as CSV
Data table columns: Date+Time, Customer, Cylinder(s), Qty, Total Amount, Paid, Due, Status Badge, Actions
Actions column: View icon (navigates to /sales/{id}), Collect icon (opens collect modal if due>0)
Overdue rows: entire row highlighted in light red #FCEAEA
Sort by any column — click header
Pagination: 20 rows per page
Total row at bottom: SUM of Amount, Paid, Due for current filtered view
Empty state: illustration + No sales found for this period
API: GET /sales?page=&from=&to=&payment_type=&search=

Page 5 — Sale Detail
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 5: Sale Detail  ·  Route: /sales/{id}
Full detail view of a single sale with complete payment history.
Back button: back to Sales History
Sale header card: Sale #ID, date, customer name, status badge
Items table: Cylinder type, Qty, Unit price, FIFO cost (hidden from salesman), Line total
Payment summary card: Total amount, Total paid (green), Remaining due (red if >0)
Payment history table: Date, Amount collected, Collected by, Notes — all payments ever made
If due > 0: prominent Collect Payment button — opens collect modal
Collect modal: remaining due shown large in red, amount input, Pay in Full shortcut, date, notes, Confirm
Empty cylinder status card: How many empties have been returned for this sale
Print Receipt button: opens browser print dialog with formatted receipt
API: GET /sales/{id}, POST /sales/{id}/pay

Page 6 — Outstanding Dues

All sales with unpaid amounts — the collection to-do list.
Page header: Outstanding Dues + total outstanding amount in red (large)
Summary cards row: Total Due Amount / Number of Customers / Oldest Due (days) / Average Due Age
Filter: Sort by Amount / Days Overdue / Customer name — dropdown
Filter: Show only overdue >7 days checkbox
Data table columns: Customer, Phone, Sale Date, Days Overdue, Total Sale, Paid, Remaining Due, Collect button
Days Overdue cell: green if <3 days, orange if 3-7 days, red if >7 days
Remaining Due cell: bold red
Collect button: opens inline collect modal for that row
Collect modal: same as Sale Detail collect modal
After collection: row updates in place (due reduces) or disappears if fully paid
Empty state: large green checkmark + All dues collected! Great work.
Export CSV button
API: GET /sales?has_due=1, POST /sales/{id}/pay

Page 7 — Customers
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 7: Customers  ·  Route: /customers
Full customer list with money due and empty cylinder balances.
Page header: Customers title, Add New Customer button top right
Search bar: search by name or phone number
Filter: Show only customers with outstanding dues checkbox
Data table columns: Customer Name, Phone, Total Sales (count), Total Due (red if >0), Empties Owed (count), Last Sale Date, View button
Empties Owed column: shows how many empty cylinders this customer still has at home
Rows with dues > 0: light red background
Rows with empties owed > 0: cylinder icon in orange
Row click: navigate to /customers/{id}
Add New Customer modal: Name (required), Phone (required), Address (optional), Notes
API: GET /customers, POST /customers

Page 8 — Customer Detail
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 8: Customer Detail  ·  Route: /customers/{id}
Complete history for one customer — sales, payments, empties.
Customer header card: name, phone, address, total due in red, empties owed in orange, Edit button
Tab navigation: Sales History / Payment History / Empty Cylinders / Notes
Tab 1 Sales History: full table of all sales to this customer — same as /sales but filtered
Tab 2 Payment History: all due_collections records for this customer with dates and amounts
Tab 3 Empty Cylinders: table showing per cylinder type — Sold to customer / Returned / Pending empties
Pending empties table row: cylinder type, qty sold total, qty returned total, qty still with customer
Mark empty returned button: quick-log that customer returned an empty for a specific cylinder type
Tab 4 Notes: free text notes history for this customer — add note button
Customer balance summary sidebar card: Total purchased ever, Total paid, Total due
API: GET /customers/{id}, GET /sales?customer_id=, GET /customers/{id}/empties

Page 9 — Empty Cylinders
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 9: Empty Cylinders  ·  Route: /empties
Log all empty cylinder collections — normal and extra.
Page header: Empty Cylinders title, Log Empty Return button top right
Summary cards: Total Empties Collected Today / Total Empties This Week / Pending Verification (extras)
Stock snapshot table: each cylinder type with filled qty / empty in warehouse / with customers
Log Empty Return button: opens modal with two tabs — Normal Return and Extra Return
Normal Return modal: Cylinder type (dropdown), Customer (searchable), Qty, Date, Notes — Submit
Extra Return modal: same fields + Reason dropdown (Old stock / Neighbour collection / Competitor cylinder / Salesman handover / Other) + extended Notes
Extra return warning text: Admin will be notified for verification
Today&apos;s Collections table: Time, Customer, Cylinder type, Qty, Type (Normal/Extra), Status (Verified/Pending)
Extra returns show EXTRA badge in purple — Pending verification in orange
Full history tab: all empty returns ever with filters
Per-customer pending empties table: which customers still have empty shells
API: GET /stock, POST /returns, POST /returns/extra

Page 10 — End of Day
<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="0F1E35" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="D0D5DD" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="F4F6F8" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="180"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="100"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="40" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0F1E35"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Page 10: End of Day  ·  Route: /eod
Daily reconciliation. One wizard per allocation. Submit before midnight.
Page header: End of Day — [today&apos;s date]
Allocation cards: one card per allocation today — each shows current status
Card states: Pending (orange) / In Progress / Reconciled (green tick)
Click on any pending card: opens the reconciliation form for that allocation
Reconciliation form (in-page panel, not modal — big enough for desktop):
  Allocation summary row at top: Cylinder type, Allocated qty, Current sold_qty from sales
  Step 1 — Confirm Sold Qty: pre-filled from actual sales, editable with warning if mismatch
  Step 2 — Empty Cylinders: split into Regular (from today sales) and Extra (others collected)
  Step 3 — Cash Submitted: amount input, pre-filled from paid sales total, editable
  Preview table: Allocated / Sold / Regular Empties / Extra Empties / Unsold (auto-calc) / Cash
  Unsold cylinders auto-calculated and shown: these will return to warehouse stock
  Warning if cash submitted is significantly less than expected (>10% gap)
  Submit Reconciliation button — teal, disabled until all fields filled
After submission: card turns green with Reconciled badge — cannot re-open
If all allocations reconciled: full-page success state with summary of the day
API: POST /allocations/{id}/reconcile

Page 11 — My Reports

Personal performance report for the salesman.
Page header: My Reports title + Date range picker (default: this month)
Quick period tabs: Today / This Week / This Month / Custom
Performance metrics row: 4 cards — Revenue / Units Sold / Cash Collected / Outstanding Dues
Chart 1: Daily Sales Bar Chart — bars show revenue per day in selected period
Chart 2: Payment Type Pie Chart — Cash vs Partial vs Due breakdown
Chart 3: Empty Cylinders Line Chart — empties collected per day
Sales breakdown table: per cylinder type — allocated total, sold total, sell-through %, revenue
Due collection table: total dues created vs total collected vs still outstanding
Empty cylinder summary table: Normal collected / Extra collected / Total
End of Day record table: each day — reconciled time, sold qty, cash submitted, status
Export all data as CSV button
API: GET /salesmen/{id}/report?from=&to=


Empty Cylinder Web Feature — Complete Flow

<w:tblPr><w:tblW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideH w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/><w:insideV w:val="single" w:color="auto" w:sz="4"/></w:tblBorders></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="9360"/></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcW w:type="dxa" w:w="9360"/><w:tcBorders><w:top w:val="single" w:color="12929C" w:sz="1"/><w:left w:val="single" w:color="12929C" w:sz="18"/><w:bottom w:val="single" w:color="12929C" w:sz="1"/><w:right w:val="single" w:color="12929C" w:sz="1"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="DFF3F5" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/><w:left w:type="dxa" w:w="200"/><w:bottom w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/><w:right w:type="dxa" w:w="120"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="60" w:before="0"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:rPr><w:rFonts w:ascii="Arial" w:cs="Arial" w:eastAsia="Arial" w:hAnsi="Arial"/><w:b/><w:bCs/><w:color w:val="0B6E75"/><w:sz w:val="20"/><w:szCs w:val="20"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Why This Needs Special Attention
Empty cylinder collection is a critical asset tracking feature.
The web app gives salesmen the most complete tool to log, track, and verify empties.
Two types: Normal returns (expected) and Extra returns (unexpected — needs admin approval).
Every empty cylinder is worth TK 300-500 as a physical asset.
